32   protected[quality] def literalsNeeded(pos: Int, typ: String = "String"): Nothing =
33     if (pos == -1)
34       qualityException("Cannot setup Quality Expression with non-literals")
35     else
36       qualityException(s"Quality Expression requires a $typ literal in (starts with position 0) position $pos")
37 
38   protected[quality] def literalsNeeded: Nothing = literalsNeeded( -1 )
39 
40   protected[quality] def getLong(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
41     exp match {
42       case Literal(seed: Long, LongType) => seed
43       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Long")
44     }
45   protected[quality] def getInteger(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
46     exp match {
47       case Literal(seed: Int, IntegerType) => seed
48       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Integer")
49     }
50   protected[quality] def getBoolean(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
51     exp match {
52       case Literal(seed: Boolean, BooleanType) => seed
53       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Boolean")
54     }
55   protected[quality] def getString(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
56     exp match {
57       case Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) => str.toString()
58       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos)
59     }
60 
61   protected[quality] def getBinary(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1): Array[Byte] =
62     exp match {
63       case Literal(ar: Array[Byte], _: BinaryType) => ar
64       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Binary")
65     }

67   protected[quality] val mustKeepNames = Set(LambdaFunctions.PlaceHolder,
68     LambdaFunctions.Lambda, LambdaFunctions.CallFun)

89   private val noopAdd = (dt: DataType) => None
90 
91   /**
92    * Provides the default monoidal add for a dataType, used for merging summed results when aggregating
93    *
94    * @param dataType
95    * @return
96    */
97   def defaultAdd(dataType: DataType, extension: DataType => Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] = noopAdd): Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] =
98     dataType match {
99       case _: MapType => Some((left, right) => MapMerge(Seq(left, right), (dataType: DataType) => defaultAdd(dataType, extension)))
100       case _: IntegerType | LongType | DoubleType =>
101         Some((left, right) => add(left, right, null))
102       case a: DecimalType =>
103         Some((left, right) => add(left, right, a))
104       case _ => extension(dataType)
105     }
106 
107   /**
108    * Provides the default monoidal Zero for a dataType, used for defaults when aggregating
109    *
110    * @param dataType
111    * @return
112    */
113   def defaultZero(dataType: DataType): Option[Any] =
114     dataType match {
115       case _: MapType => Some(EmptyMap)
116       case _: IntegerType | LongType => Some(0L)
117       case _: DoubleType => Some(0.0)
118       case d: DecimalType => Some(Decimal.createUnsafe(0, d.precision, d.scale))
119       case _ => None
120     }
121 
122   /**
123    * Wrap to provide the default lookup for registerFunctions to change type parsing from DDL based to other or
124    * when None to add additional lookups should ddl fail
125    *
126    * @param string
127    * @return
128    */
129   def defaultParseTypes(string: String): Option[DataType] =
130     try {
131       Some(
132         DataType.fromDDL(string)
133       )
134     } catch {
135       case _: Throwable => None
136     }
137 
138   // #12 - use underscore names but keep the old camel case approach around for compat
139   def registerWithChecks(registerFunction: (String, Seq[Expression] => Expression) => Unit, name: String, argsf: Seq[Expression] => Expression, paramNumbers: Set[Int] = Set.empty, minimum: Int = -1) = {
140     val create =
141       if (paramNumbers.isEmpty && minimum == -1 )
142         argsf
143       else
144         (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
145           if ((paramNumbers.nonEmpty && !paramNumbers.contains(exps.size)) || (minimum > exps.size)) {
146             val sizeerr =
147               if (paramNumbers.nonEmpty)
148                 s"Valid parameter counts are ${paramNumbers.mkString(", ")}"
149               else
150                 s"A minimum of $minimum parameters is required."
151             throw QualityException(s"Wrong number of arguments provided to Quality function $name, received ${exps.size}. $sizeerr")
152           }
153           argsf(exps)
154         }
155 
156     registerFunction(name, create)
157 
158     if (!mustKeepNames(name)) {
159       registerFunction(name.replaceAll("_",""), create)
160     }
161   }
162 
163   /**
164    * Must be called before using any functions like Passed, Failed or Probability(X)
165    * @param parseTypes override type parsing (e.g. DDL, defaults to defaultParseTypes / DataType.fromDDL)
166    * @param zero override zero creation for aggExpr (defaults to defaultZero)
167    * @param add override the "add" function for aggExpr types (defaults to defaultAdd(dataType))
168    * @param writer override the printCode and printExpr print writing function (defaults to println)
169    * @param registerFunction function to register the sql extensions
170    */
171   def registerQualityFunctions(parseTypes: String => Option[DataType] = defaultParseTypes,
172                                zero: DataType => Option[Any] = defaultZero,
173                                add: DataType => Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] = (dataType: DataType) => defaultAdd(dataType),
174                                mapCompare: DataType => Option[(Any, Any) => Int] = (dataType: DataType) => utils.defaultMapCompare(dataType),
175                                writer: String => Unit = println,
176                                registerFunction: (String, Seq[Expression] => Expression) => Unit =
177                                 (n, f) => ShimUtils.registerFunction(SparkSession.active)(n,f)
178                               ) : Unit = {
179 
180     def register(name: String, argsf: Seq[Expression] => Expression, paramNumbers: Set[Int] = Set.empty, minimum: Int = -1) =
181       registerWithChecks(registerFunction, name, argsf, paramNumbers, minimum)
182 
183     def parse(exp: Expression) = {
184       val Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) = exp // only accept type as string
185       parseTypes(str.toString).getOrElse(qualityException(s"Could not parse the type $str"))
186     }
187 
188     def getMap(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) = exp match {
189       case l: Literal if l.dataType.isInstanceOf[MapType] =>
190         MapUtils.toScalaMap(l.value.asInstanceOf[ArrayBasedMapData], StringType, StringType).map(p => (p._1.toString, p._2.toString))
191       case c: CreateMap if c.children.grouped(2).forall{
192         case Seq(Literal(_: UTF8String, StringType), _: Literal) =>
193           true
194         case _ => false
195       } =>
196         c.children.grouped(2).map{
197           case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), value: Literal) =>
198             str.toString -> value.value.toString()
199         }.toMap
200       case _ => throw QualityException(s"Could not process a literal map with expression $exp index $pos")
201     }

247     def strType(exp: Expression) = {

252     register(LambdaFunctions.PlaceHolder, {
253       case Seq(e, Literal(bol: Boolean, BooleanType)) =>
254         PlaceHolderExpression(parse(e), bol)
255       case Seq(e) =>
256         PlaceHolderExpression(parse(e))
257       case _ =>
258         PlaceHolderExpression(LongType)
259     }, Set(0, 1, 2))

261     /* Note - both Lambda and CallFun are only called in top level expressions,
262           nested calls are handled within the lambda expression
263           creation that "calls" this. */
264 
265     register(LambdaFunctions.Lambda, {
266       case Seq(fun: FunForward) =>
267         val res = FunCall(fun)
268         res
269       case Seq(fun: FunN) =>
270         // placeholders that are 1:1
271         val res = fun.function
272         res
273       case e => qualityException(s"Quality _lambda_ can only be used with Quality User Functions, but was called with $e")
274     }, Set(1))
275 
276     register(LambdaFunctions.CallFun, {
277       case (fun@ FunN(_, l@ SLambdaFunction(ff : FunForward, _, _), _, _, _, _)) +: args =>
278         processTopCallFun(fun, l, ff, args)
279       case t => qualityException(s"${LambdaFunctions.CallFun} should only be used to process partially applied functions returned by a user lambda, got $t instead")
280     }, minimum = 1)

282     val afx = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
283       val (sumType, filter, sum, count) =
284         exps.size match {
285           case 3 =>
286             // attempt to take a look at exps1 to identify if it's a FunN or mapWith
287             val typ =
288               exps(1) match {
289                 case FunN(Seq(RefExpression(dataType, _, _)), _, _, _, _, _) => dataType // would default to long anyway
290                 case MapTransform(RefExpression(t: MapType, _, _), _, _, _) => t
291                 case _ => LongType
292               }
293             (typ, exps(0), exps(1), exps(2))
294           case 4 =>
295             val Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) = exps(0) // only accept type as string
296             if (str.toString == "NO_REWRITE")
297             // signal not to replace types
298             (null, exps(1), exps(2), exps(3))
299               else
300               (parse(exps(0)), exps(1), exps(2), exps(3))
301         }
302       AggregateExpressions(sumType, filter, sum, count, zero, add)
303     }
304     register("agg_Expr", afx, Set(3, 4))
305 
306     register("sum_With", (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
307       val (dataType, origExp) = exps.size match {
308         case 1 => (LongType, exps(0))
309         // backwards compat
310         case 2 => (parse(exps(0)), exps(1))
311       }
312       FunN(Seq(RefExpression(dataType)), origExp, Some("sum_With"), usedAsLambda = true)
313     }, Set(1, 2))
314 
315     val ff2 = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
316       // real type for param1 is changed by aggrExpr, but last works for all compat as well
317       val (sumType, exp) =
318         exps.size match {
319           case 1 => (LongType, exps(0))
320           case 2 => (parse(exps(0)), exps(1)) // support the NO_REWRITE override case
321         }
322 
323       FunN(Seq(RefExpression(sumType), RefExpression(LongType)), exp, Some("results_With"), usedAsLambda = true)
324     }
325     register("results_With", ff2, Set(1, 2))
326 
327     val mapFX = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => exps.size match {
328       case 3 =>
329         // parse it for old sql to support backwards aggExpr
330         MapTransform.create(RefExpression(parse(exps(0))), exps(1), exps(2), zero)
331       case 2 =>
332         // default to LongType, aggrExpr must fix, 2nd param is key, third the func manipulating the key
333         MapTransform.create(RefExpression(MapType(LongType, LongType)), exps(0), exps(1), zero)
334     }
335     register("map_With", mapFX, Set(2, 3))

337     def aggFWith(fun: String) = (what: String) => (exps: Seq[Expression]) => expression(
338       if (exps.size == 0)
339         functions.expr(s"$fun( $what )")
340       else
341         functions.expr(s"$fun('${strType(exps(0))}', $what )")
342       )
343 
344     val retWith = aggFWith("results_With")
345 
346     // common cases
347     register("meanF", retWith("(sum, count) -> sum / count"), Set(0, 1))
348 
349     val sumWith = aggFWith("sum_With")

351     val incX = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => exps match {
352       case Seq(x: AttributeReference) =>
353         val name = x.qualifier.mkString(".") + x.name // that is bad code man should be option
354         sumWith(s"sum -> sum + $name")(Seq())
355       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType)) =>
356         // case for type passing
357         sumWith("sum -> sum + 1")(exps)
358       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), x: AttributeReference) =>
359         val name = x.qualifier.mkString(".") + x.name
360         sumWith(s"sum -> sum + $name")(Seq(exps(0))) // keep the type, drop the attr
361       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), y) =>
362         qualityException(INC_REWRITE_GENEXP_ERR_MSG)
363       case Seq( y ) =>
364         val SLambdaFunction(a: Add, Seq(sum: UnresolvedNamedLambdaVariable), hidden ) = expression(functions.expr("sumWith(sum -> sum + 1)")).children(0)
365         import ShimUtils.{add => addf}
366         // could be a cast around x or three attributes plusing each other or....
367         FunN(Seq(RefExpression(LongType)),
368           SLambdaFunction(addf(a.left, y, LongType), Seq(sum), hidden )
369           , Some("inc"), usedAsLambda = true) // keep the type
370       case Seq() => expression(functions.expr(s"sumWith(sum -> sum + 1)"))
371     }
372     register("inc", incX, Set(1, 0, 2))
373 
374     // return sum
375     register("return_Sum", retWith("(sum, count) -> sum"), Set(0, 1))
376     def getRandom(exp: Expression, pos: Int) = {
377       val str = getString(exp, pos)
378       RandomSource.valueOf(str)
379     }

381     // random generators
382     val brf = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
383       //numBytes: Int, randomSource: RandomSource, seed: Long constructor but needs to use random, seed, numbytes
384       val (numBytes: Int, randomSource, seed: Long) =
385         exps.size match {
386           case 0 => (16, RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L)
387           case 1 => (16, getRandom(exps(0), 0), 0L)
388           case 2 => (16, getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
389           case 3 => (getInteger(exps(2), 2), getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
390           case _ => literalsNeeded
391         }
392 
393       RandomBytes(numBytes, randomSource, seed)
394     }
395     register("rng_Bytes", brf, Set(0,1,2,3))
396 
397     // random generators
398     val lrf = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
399 
400       //randomSource: RandomSource, seed: Long constructor but needs to use random, seed, numbytes
401       val (randomSource, seed: Long) =
402         exps.size match {
403           case 0 => (RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L)
404           case 1 => (getRandom(exps(0), 0), 0L)
405           case 2 => (getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
406           case _ => literalsNeeded
407         }
408 
409       RandomLongs.create(randomSource, seed)
410     }
411     register("rng", lrf, Set(0,1,2))

430     val longPairEqual = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
431       val Seq(Literal(a, StringType), Literal(b, StringType)) = exps
432 
433       def lower(a: Any) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_lower")
434 
435       def higher(a: Any) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_higher")
436 
437       And(EqualTo(lower(a), lower(b)), EqualTo(higher(a), higher(b)))
438     }
439     register("long_Pair_Equal", longPairEqual, Set(2))
440 
441     val idEqual = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
442       val Seq(Literal(a, StringType), Literal(b, StringType)) = exps
443       def attr(a: Any, field: String) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_$field")
444 
445       And(And(EqualTo(attr(a, "base"), attr(b, "base")),
446         EqualTo(attr(a, "i0"), attr(b, "i0"))),
447         EqualTo(attr(a, "i1"), attr(b, "i1")))
448     }
449     register("id_equal", idEqual, Set(2))

455     def fieldsToHash(asStruct: Boolean, factory: String => DigestFactory) = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
456       exps.size match {
457         case a if a > 3 =>
458           val digestImpl = getString(exps(0), 0)
459 
460           HashFunctionsExpression(exps.drop(1), digestImpl, asStruct, factory(digestImpl))
461 
462         case _ => literalsNeeded
463       }
464 
465     register("digest_To_Longs_Struct", fieldsToHash(true, MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 2)
466     register("digest_To_Longs",  fieldsToHash(false, MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 2)
467 
468     def fieldBasedID(factory: String => DigestFactory) = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
469       exps.size match {
470         case a if a > 3 =>
471           val digestImpl = getString(exps(1), 1)
472           val prefix = getString(exps.head, 0)
473 
474           GenericLongBasedIDExpression(model.FieldBasedID,
475             HashFunctionsExpression(exps.drop(2), digestImpl, true, factory(digestImpl)), prefix)
476 
477         case _ => literalsNeeded
478       }

577       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), e: Expression) =>

598     register("qualityfunn", exps => {
599       val args = exps.dropRight(4)
600       val params = exps.drop(args.length)
601       val (function, name, attemptCodeGen, useAsLambda) =
602         params match {
603           case Seq(f, n, a, u) => (f, {
604             val s = getString(n)
605             if (s.isEmpty)
606               None
607             else
608               Some(s)
609           }, getBoolean(a), getBoolean(u))
610         }
611       FunN(args, function, name, attemptCodeGen = attemptCodeGen, usedAsLambda = useAsLambda)
612     }, minimum = 5)
